$(function () {
	
// Move Modal to Body
var modal = $('#modal-window');
modal.appendTo('body'); // at the end of the Body

removeCssRule();
})

function removeCssRule(){
	if(site_id === 80){
		var css = document.getElementById('wp-custom-css');
		if(css !== undefined && css.type === 'text/css'){
			css.sheet.deleteRule(53); // .ui-datepicker
		}
	}
}
